# The Phantom of the Mist

## FOG-000 Prologue

?? ?? N.F. 1??

[DISMYTH] [Chat Room] [Has anyone seen the strange shadows in the fog?]

Legend Hunter R.C. [OP]: Have you guys heard? Sloane Street's covered in fog again.

Legend Hunter R.C. [OP]: Normal? Ha, absolutely not. There's so little rain in DisCity. Why would there be fog? Unless...

Legend Hunter R.C. [OP]: Yes, "that" must be it. The long-lost antagonist of that urban legend is back...

...The Mad Shears of the Foggy Night is coming to hunt again...

A maze of dark alleys... Tonight's prey is running, desperately trying to flee the rolling fog creeping up from behind, and the terrifying voice ringing out from within...

Snap... snap...

The sound of footsteps... no, the sound of snapping shears... echoes from the depths of the fog.

No matter where the prey flees, it follows like a shadow, snapping rhythmically as it closes in on the prey.

If at this moment, you dare to turn your head, and peek at the true face of that ghastly shadow...

<ins>The man being chased suddenly looks back, yet what can he see in the thick fog?</ins><br><ins>The woman being chased suddenly looks back, yet what can she see in the thick fog?</ins>

<ins>Interference noises keep crackling from the comms device in his hand. He can vaguely hear a woman calling for him, but he doesn't dare answer. Panting heavily, he remains on high alert.</ins><br><ins>Interference noises keep crackling from the comms device in her hand. She can vaguely hear a woman calling her, but she doesn't dare answer. Panting heavily, she remains on high alert.</ins>

The sound of snapping shears can still be heard. That "person" is still nearby. Can't stop, keep running! Hurry! Must escape from here.

How can it be seen? It's Mad Shears, after all.

No one knows who it is, what it is, why it attacks, or what strange powers it has.

Mad Shears has been unleashing attacks in Eastside for several years now. Even after being followed and tracked for so long, no clues were ever found.

...Then, why is it that no one can find clues about Mad Shears?

<ins>The fog is still all around, but suddenly, the heavy chimes of a clock tower echo through the misty depths, reaching that poor wretch's ears. He's almost at the edge of the fog. He's saved!</ins><br><ins>The fog is still all around, but suddenly, the heavy chimes of a clock tower echo through the misty depths, reaching that poor wretch's ears. She's almost at the edge of the fog. She's saved!</ins>

The runner takes a deep breath and tries to make a last sprint.

But it's too late.

Of course, it can't be found because...

<ins>The fog envelops the poor escapee. Something presses intimately against his back.</ins><br><ins>The fog envelops the poor escapee. Something presses intimately against her back.</ins>

Everyone it targets...

Everyone who targets it...

Yes, myself included...

<ins>The man whips out a gun, but before he can aim it, the barrel gets sliced off.</ins><br><ins>The woman whips out a gun, but before she can aim it, the barrel gets sliced off.</ins>

Our **** have all been cut out by Mad Shears...

The hunter is within reach. Yet what the prey sees first is a long, cold blade swinging forth, accompanied by an icy, murderous grin...

Chief: ... It's not time to surrender yet!

Serious Woman: Chief!!!

<ins>In a split second, the spike of red light is sliced apart. His brain hums as if he had suffered a blunt blow to his head...</ins><br><ins>In a split second, the spike of red light is sliced apart. Her brain hums as if she had suffered a blunt blow to her head...</ins>

The impact severely impairs your consciousness. Amidst the chaos, you're spurred on only by raw instinct. You struggle and try to crawl out of the fog and dark alley with all your might.

But all the strength in your body disappears alongside the shackles in your palms, and your vision gradually blurs.

??: ...

Chief: Who is that...

You don't even have the strength to lift up your head. You can barely make out the voice of the person who came. All signals you pick up are immediately shattered by your collapsing consciousness.

??: ...What... happened to you...

You open your mouth to say something, but forget what you're saying. As that person leans toward you...

Your last bit of consciousness gets swallowed up by the fog.

█ Number: FAC-M-122-I

█ File: Eastside Contamination Monitoring Log

*Entry I:

The M-value of DisCity began to fluctuate once more, and Mania incidents reappeared. To fight the contamination, the City Council passed a motion to activate rainmaking machines that produce blue rain.

While the city's M-value was brought under control, strange fog began to appear in various parts of the city.

All 26 cases of fog occurred at night, and each did not exceed three hours.

*Entry II:

The thick fog might be a side effect of blue rain purification, but it might also be from an unidentified Mania incident.

The FAC has dispatched an inspector to Sloane Street to investigate.

*Annex V — Incident Aftermath:

On the day of the investigation, Sloane Street was once again overtaken by dense fog. Rescue efforts failed and the mission was aborted.

Contact was lost, and the inspector's whereabouts remain unknown.

## FOG-101 Unfamiliar Room

You're running in a dark alley, with no beginning and no end.

Amidst the heavy shadows, something ensnares you in your tracks, tearing open the dream...

Chief: !

A light, fuzzy blanket is draped over your body. It feels soft but unfamiliar, and the fabric exudes a reassuring fragrance.

**【Advance】**

- Try to get up

**end**

You're lying on a soft bed. The light blanket falls to the floor from your movement.

The clean, gentle fragrance in the room reminds you of sunlit flowers and freshly baked bread... There's also a faint whiff of gel spray.

**【Advance】**

- Look around

**end**

You're alone in the room, but it has clearly been tidied up by someone else.

You look in the mirror. You seem to have been tidied up, too. The clothes on your body are clean and dry, and they smell the same as the room.

As you turn around carefully, your fingers touch something—a comms device with a broken screen.

**【Advance】**

- Try to turn it on

**end**

It flickers, then lights up meekly. The screen is stuck on a strange-looking website.

*[DISMYTH] [Chat Room] [Mad Shears is here? The fog is spreading... the horrific urban legend might come true!!]*

Chief: Hmm...

It's a strange chat room on a website you've never seen before. You have no recollection of the text there.

**【Advance】**

- Look elsewhere

**end**

There's a note under a glass of water on the table next to the sofa.

"Morning.

I didn't wake you up before I left because of how late things dragged out last night. You should catch up on your sleep.

Though I didn't have time to make you breakfast, I left some bread and jam on the table for you to nibble on.

I'm going to work now. Rest well and wait for me to come home."

The note also smells familiar. Your memory wavers. You don't seem to remember anything, yet feel oddly at peace. You vaguely feel that there's nothing unusual about this situation.

Better eat and tidy up first before going out. Even if you are to investigate, you should start with this little house.

## FOG-102 Fog and Hair Monster

The chiming of clock bells echoes through the streets as you walk past the next alley.

Ding... Dong... The clock continues to chime. You pass by a few residents as you silently count the number of chimes.

One, two, three... nine, ten, eleven...

Twelve, thirteen...?

The chimes end abruptly on this odd number. At the same time, you hear a bloodcurdling scream from not far away.

Resident: Ahhh...?!!

A gust of cold, damp air brushes past you. It came from behind you, from the end of the alley.

Resident: W-what's that?

An ominous gray mist is spreading, with eerie strand-like creatures squirming from inside.

They wriggle and coil before finally standing upright one by one, transforming into strange monsters!

??: Roarrrr... Roarrrr...!!

They stretch out their "hands" and drag screaming passers-by into the mist. The next second, the thick fog engulfs the sky, and night falls upon the entire street.

**【Advance】**

- Run!

**end**

Terror and mist soon envelops everything, catching everyone off guard. Only a few sources of light remain in the distance.

Resident: Ah...?!

Someone falls. The monsters will not miss this chance.

Resident: Mad Shears?! St-stay, stay back!

**【Choose One】**

- Go help

  However, the monsters are clearly stronger. The next second, the girl is dragged into the fog, leaving only the image of a terrified face imprinted on your retinas.

- Keep running

  The next second, the girl is dragged into the fog, leaving only the image of a terrified face imprinted on your retinas.

**end**

As the girl gets dragged away, you catch a glimpse of what's wrapped around her body: long, dark and agile strands, brimming with eerie life.

**【Advance】**

- What was that?!

**end**

...It was the shine of human hair.

## FOG-103 Shears in the Alley

Visibility doesn't exceed ten meters right now. The monsters are prowling in the thick fog, ready to devour their next victim.

Chief: Ha... ha...

You stumble and fall into a dark, narrow alley, merging into its shadows.

The monsters in the alley don't attack you. Instead, they back away rather fearfully.

**【Advance】**

- Raise your hand

**end**

Upon seeing your movement, they back away a bit more in disgust. They appear to acting out of intelligence, but also out of instinct—for some unknown or forgotten reason, they're avoiding you.

**【Advance】**

- Find a safe place to hide.

**end**

The monsters back away, allowing you to retreat further down the alley. The fog here is damp and cold, making the alley seem even more dark and sinister.

...However, this dark alley feels familiar to you.

Suddenly...

...Snap...

...Snap, snap...

The cold, familiar feeling spreads swiftly along your spine to the back of your head. The sound is coming from the depths of the fog, but also from the depths of your memory.

You've been here before. You fought someone here.

Alongside your headache, fragments of memory are stirring in your mind. The dark alley, the thick fog, the clock bells, and the incessant... snapping sounds?

Resident: Mad Shears?! St-stay, stay back!

Snap.

Memories overlap with reality as the snapping sound of shears edges closer.

There's a figure in the depths of the fog. Obscured by mist, its limbs appear slender and fragile. At the same time, that giant pair of shears is scraping against the wall, emitting hungry crunching sounds.

Mad Shears: I found...

Mad Shears: I found you!

## FOG-L1 Guiding Light 1

Snip, snip, snip...

Snip, snip, snip...

The sound is hard on your heels like a bloodhound. You've been fumbling around in the dark, walled in by thick fog, for God knows how long, but still can't find an exit.

It is getting ever closer. At any minute, you expect to look up and see the mysterious being behind the sound reveal itself in front of you.

Snip, snip, snip...

You now find yourself in a narrow alley, with nowhere to hide.

Everything is coming to a head...

You feel a tap on your shoulder.

It's a tap on your shoulder, you're sure.

??: Quick... this way...

**【Advance】**

- Hey!

**end**

You turn around. You see ahead of you a patch of light bobbing along in the fog.

It stops, as if waiting for you to catch up.

**【Advance】**

- Catch up with the light

**end**

You don't know if it bodes well or ill. But one thing is certain; it's able to move freely in the fog.

You decide to risk it. Who knows? Maybe you'll be able to get out of this hellish place by following the light.

## FOG-L2 Guiding Light 2

Snip, snip, snip...

With the snipping sound still behind you, you quicken your pace to catch up with the light.

You could use some company, if nothing else.

The light seems to prefer to keep you at a distance.

## FOG-L3 Guiding Light 3

Every time you get too close, or try to reach out and touch it, it flickers and vanishes, only to reappear a short distance ahead.

Its behavior brings to mind the ghost story that has been going around in this town.

It's rumored that the town is haunted by an evil phantom that wanders in the dark and plays tricks on innocent passers-by.

Still, in comparison to the mysterious being behind you, whoever or whatever is controlling the light seems much less menacing.

It might prove to be the lesser of two evils.

You hold your breath and cautiously follow the bobbing light while keeping constant track of the snipping sound.

At least the light is moving away from the wall of fog behind you.

## FOG-L4 Guiding Light 4

??: It's... getting brighter...

The light stops moving. It's a lantern, and you finally have a chance to see who or what is holding it.

By the light of the lantern, all you can see is a fuzzy shadow figure.

Snip, snip, snip...

The familiar yet haunting sound is approaching fast.

The shadow figure leaps into the air and breaks open the wall of fog in front of you. You follow it out of the fog.

## FOG-104 Mad Shears' Despair

You finally break out of the fog. The light that guided you flickers twice inside the fog, then leaps into a blind zone.

The sound of shears continues to linger in the fog, refusing to give up the chase.

Lantern: Here... Over here! Hurry...

Street lights still illuminate the main road, making it still possible to tell the direction.

As you try to hide, you catch sight of a strange tricolor "street light" out of the corner of your eye.

Yet in that moment of distraction, you get knocked to the ground by the monster. With a flash of cold light, the shears pin down your collar.

Mad Shears: My shears... My shears...

Mad Shears: It's was you... You took my shears...

It twists and wriggles, as if trying to break free from some kind of pain. The shear blades carve deep crevices into the ground alongside its movements.

**【Advance】**

- I don't remember doing something like that!

**end**

Chief: I don't remember you at all!

Mad Shears: ...You don't remember?

Mad Shears: You don't remember?!! How can you not remember?!! I can't forget any of it... how can you?! Ahhh how can you?!

Mad Shears: Just as I thought, you have my shears. You stole them from me. You freed yourself, but what about me?!

Mad Shears: I'm still entangled. I can't cut it off no matter what. It's all your fault!

You start to get angry at all the false accusations.

**【Advance】**

- I don't have any shears!

**end**

Chief: Look at what's in your hands! And you've the gall to demand things from an empty-handed person like me?

Mad Shears: The shears in my hand recognize you! The shears say it's you!

More slashes come your way. It's completely ignoring your words, eyes brimming with resentment and madness. It's pointless to try to talk to it. You can only dodge and pray for the best.

Mad Shears: Where'd you hide it? Where'd you hide it?

Mad Shears: In your stomach? In your head? Let me slice them open! I know you have it!

It roars and rushes at you, but when you instinctively raise up your hand towards it, the crazed maniac stops in its tracks.

Mad Shears: What? (*Gasps)

Its sudden frightened expression emboldens you.

**【Advance】**

- I won't lose!

**end**

Nothing happened. Your hand is suspended awkwardly in mid-air. You're not sure why you did this. The other party is also a bit surprised.

Mad Shears: ...

Chief: ...

Chief: I told you already... I don't remember anything...

After a brief silence, you spring into action before the she does.

**【Choose One】**

- Run!

- Look behind you! Shears!

**end**

You yell and point behind it, then turn and dash the other way.

But you barely take one step before tripping and falling to the ground. In the darkness, a strange strand of hair has wrapped itself around your ankle.

Mad Shears: You can't escape! You're no different! You can't escape, either!! Ahhhh...

Ting-a-ling...

The light behind you suddenly lights up.

Mad Shears: Ah... Ahhhhh...!

You slowly open your eyes. The fog is rapidly fading away. The bizarre hair creatures are scattering in all directions, like insects fleeing the light.

Mad Shears is no exception. It shrieks angrily and vanishes with the fog.

Someone opens the door of the shop behind you. The melodious sound of copper chimes rings in your ears.

??: ...Why are you here?

??: Didn't I tell you to rest at home and wait for me to get off work?

What a familiar voice. A faint fragrance soothes your panicked breathing.

The woman has already walked up to you. She reaches out to stroke your disheveled hair, her voice holds a trace of sympathy.

??: ...You made a big mess of yourself again. You can't go back looking like this...

??: Come, give me your hand. Let me fix you up before going home.

## FOG-105 Nice Hairdresser

She leads you to the shop with the tricolor light, pushes open the door, and steps into its warm glow from the dark night.

It's a small hair salon. On the side of the door, where employee nameplates are meant to be hung, there's only one small metal nameplate engraved with a cursive signature:

**【Advance】**

- ...Adela?

**end**

Gentle Woman: Yes, I'm here.

The woman named Adela looks back at you. You study her face carefully. You don't recognize it, yet it seems familiar at the same time. You're certain that she must exist in some corner of your shattered memories.

**【Advance】**

- ...I've seen you before.

**end**

Adela: Ha, what a funny thing to say. You make it sound as if you've forgotten who I am. This is the second time you've fallen in front of me.

Adela: Last night I brought you home, and today I brought you into my salon. You keep making a big mess of yourself... and I have to work overtime to tidy you up.

She reaches out and strokes your hair, which had been completely messed up by the monster. She then fixes your crumpled shirt.

Adela: At least you didn't pass out this time. You can change into clean clothes on your own.

Adela: That was the hardest thing for me last night.

She chuckles. You blush, trying not to imagine her changing your clothes.

Now that she mentioned it, the clothes you have on right now are indeed similar to Adela's in style and color. You're a foot away from her, yet totally enveloped by that familiar and reassuring fragrance.

—So the fragrance turned out to be hers. It dispelled your nightmare this morning, and now it's soothing your panic.

You think of the warm water by the bed, the meticulously treated wounds, the hasty but fulfilling breakfast, the washed clothes, and the fact that such a slender girl carried your unconscious body all the way home from the back alley...

**【Advance】**

- You're the one who saved me.

**end**

Chief: Thank you. Sorry for troubling you.

Adela: No worries. I'm good at taking care of others. The most important thing was to get you to sleep well.

Adela: Now that you came to my little salon, you're my "customer."

As she says this, she brings you to a leather recliner to sit, then puts her hands on your shoulders and slowly presses you to lie down. She does this very gently, yet firmly enough that you can't refuse.

You lie down flat, your head hanging over the small sink. Adela goes to the other side of the sink. She gently supports your head with her soft hands and gazes down into your eyes.

Adela: Let's rinse first. Your nerves are too wound up. You need to relax.

Adela: Tell me whatever you want to say. Take your time. I always satisfy my customers' needs.

Adela: All right, close your eyes. Don't let the bubbles get in them.

The warm water flows over your head, gently seeping through your hair before forming a downflowing stream.

Adela: How's the water temperature?

**【Choose One】**

- Quite comfy, thanks.

- A bit too cold.

  Adela: Sorry. How about now?

  The water becomes a bit warmer.

  **【Ask More】**

  - Quite comfy, thanks.【Exit】

  - Still cold.

        The woman looks at you with a smile. Her hands are a bit red, but she doesn't seem upset at your mischief at all.

          Your haircut is done. Adela didn't make any drastic changes. She just trimmed it a little, hiding the gaping cuts made by "Mad Shears" with clever layering. You look quite spruced up.

          The terrible snapping hallucinations gradually subside from your ears. You see your familiar old self in the mirror, even though you still can't remember a lot of things from the past.

              The barber shears hang less than two centimeters above your neck. This distance is quite intimate.

              Adela: No one knows its true appearance. Mad Shears never misses a target, so it never needs to come back for a retry... Oh, except for you, of course. You're quite special indeed.

              The barber shears move faster and faster. You feel its cold gleam edging closer, as if it's going to cut your exposed skin the next second.

              The cold, sharp touch feels so real. Every cell is screaming, but you can't tell if they're screaming for you to flee or embrace its approach. Then...

              The shears suddenly stop two centimeters from your neck, half-open and gleaming coldly. You glance up and see Adela looking at you in the mirror.

              Adela: It'll probably still try to come to you amidst the thick fog. Just like tonight.

              Clank...!!

              You instantly jump up and grab her hand that's holding the shears. By the time you realize what you did, the two of you are frozen face to face before the mirror.

              **【Choose One】**

              - ...Sorry.

              - ...I don't like shears.

              Adela looks at you without struggling. Her gaze grows softer.

              Adela: ...You had the same expression last night.

              Adela: Sorry, I didn't realize how much those shears traumatized you. I'm always quite the dullard.

              Adela: Forgetting the past makes you quite uneasy, doesn't it? Rest assured, you're not a weakling.

              She gently caresses your face with her empty left hand.

              Adela: You're strong enough to be its match. That's why you got injured all over. No matter what happened in the fog last night, Mad Shears didn't dare to keep chasing you after you left the fog. Nor did it dare to attack you when you were unconscious.

              - ...Wasn't that because you came?

              Adela: You're very strong. It's just that you can't remember—but that doesn't change who you are.

              She gently straightens your shoulders and motions for you to look in the mirror.

              Your haircut is done. Adela didn't make any drastic changes. She just trimmed it a little, hiding the gaping cuts made by "Mad Shears" with clever layering. You look quite spruced up.

              The terrible snapping hallucinations gradually subside from your ears. You see your familiar old self in the mirror, even though you still can't remember a lot of things from the past.

              After calming down, you make up your mind... Whatever your suspicions, this hair stylist just saved you right in front of "Mad Shears". It's a fact that she's not the same person as that monster.

              Your tense muscles finally relax. Only then do you remember to let go of her hand. Gazing at her calm face, you actually come to admire this woman a bit.

              - Do you want to go find it?

              **end**

              Adela: No need. Things are fine the way they are.

              Adela: My life is as peaceful as always. I'm content. It should continue like this.

              Adela: I have work tomorrow, so I can't help you. But you can explore the neighborhood after the sun comes up. The neighbors are all quite kind. Maybe someone has seen you before and can give you some clues.

      Your haircut is done. Adela didn't make any drastic changes. She just trimmed it a little, hiding the gaping cuts made by "Mad Shears" with clever layering. You look quite spruced up.

      The terrible snapping hallucinations gradually subside from your ears. You see your familiar old self in the mirror, even though you still can't remember a lot of things from the past.

Adela: The wash is done. Come, sit here. Let me trim this a bit for you.

This time, she ushers you before a mirror, puts you in a barber's chair, then blow-dries your hair. From the reflection, you watch her stare at your hair as she combs it carefully, as if designing a new hairstyle in her mind. Her warm fingertips brush against your ears and neck from time to time.

  Those petite barber shears are still dancing lightly, neither sticking to your skin, nor suddenly expanding and piercing towards your throat.

  The barber shears hang less than two centimeters above your neck. This distance is quite intimate.

  The cuts are small and the movements are light, clipping away at your hair and tugging at your tense nerves.

  Your gaze is completely fixed on the shears in the reflection.

  Adela: No one knows its true appearance. Mad Shears never misses a target, so it never needs to come back for a retry... Oh, except for you, of course. You're quite special indeed.

  The barber shears move faster and faster. You feel its cold gleam edging closer, as if it's going to cut your exposed skin the next second.

  The cold, sharp touch feels so real. Every cell is screaming, but you can't tell if they're screaming for you to flee or embrace its approach. Then...

  The shears suddenly stop two centimeters from your neck, half-open and gleaming coldly. You glance up and see Adela looking at you in the mirror.

  Adela: It'll probably still try to come to you amidst the thick fog. Just like tonight.

  Clank...!!

  You instantly jump up and grab her hand that's holding the shears. By the time you realize what you did, the two of you are frozen face to face before the mirror.

  **【Choose One】**

  - ...Sorry.

  - ...I don't like shears.

  **end**

  Adela looks at you without struggling. Her gaze grows softer.

  Adela: ...You had the same expression last night.

  Adela: Sorry, I didn't realize how much those shears traumatized you. I'm always quite the dullard.

  Adela: Forgetting the past makes you quite uneasy, doesn't it? Rest assured, you're not a weakling.

  She gently caresses your face with her empty left hand.

  Adela: You're strong enough to be its match. That's why you got injured all over. No matter what happened in the fog last night, Mad Shears didn't dare to keep chasing you after you left the fog. Nor did it dare to attack you when you were unconscious.

  **【Advance】**

  - ...Wasn't that because you came?

  **end**

  Adela: No, it fled before I came. I'm sure of it. Last night was your victory.

  Adela: You're very strong. It's just that you can't remember—but that doesn't change who you are.

  She gently straightens your shoulders and motions for you to look in the mirror.

  Your haircut is done. Adela didn't make any drastic changes. She just trimmed it a little, hiding the gaping cuts made by "Mad Shears" with clever layering. You look quite spruced up.

  The terrible snapping hallucinations gradually subside from your ears. You see your familiar old self in the mirror, even though you still can't remember a lot of things from the past.

  After calming down, you make up your mind... Whatever your suspicions, this hair stylist just saved you right in front of "Mad Shears". It's a fact that she's not the same person as that monster.

  Your tense muscles finally relax. Only then do you remember to let go of her hand. Gazing at her calm face, you actually come to admire this woman a bit.

  **【Advance】**

  - ...You're pretty impressive, too.

  **end**

  Chief: You know full well of Mad Shears' existence, yet you still dared to venture out at night alone and save me from the fog. This isn't just an urban legend. You could've gotten hurt.

  - Do you want to go find it?

  Adela: I have work tomorrow, so I can't help you. But you can explore the neighborhood after the sun comes up. The neighbors are all quite kind. Maybe someone has seen you before and can give you some clues.

Adela: They say that Mad Shears doesn't hurt the prey's body. It only harvests the prey's soul.

Adela: No one knows its true appearance. Mad Shears never misses a target, so it never needs to come back for a retry... Oh, except for you, of course. You're quite special indeed.

The barber shears move faster and faster. You feel its cold gleam edging closer, as if it's going to cut your exposed skin the next second.

The cold, sharp touch feels so real. Every cell is screaming, but you can't tell if they're screaming for you to flee or embrace its approach. Then...

The shears suddenly stop two centimeters from your neck, half-open and gleaming coldly. You glance up and see Adela looking at you in the mirror.

Adela: It'll probably still try to come to you amidst the thick fog. Just like tonight.

Clank...!!

You instantly jump up and grab her hand that's holding the shears. By the time you realize what you did, the two of you are frozen face to face before the mirror.

**【Choose One】**

- ...Sorry.

- ...I don't like shears.

**end**

Adela looks at you without struggling. Her gaze grows softer.

Adela: ...You had the same expression last night.

Adela: Sorry, I didn't realize how much those shears traumatized you. I'm always quite the dullard.

Adela: Forgetting the past makes you quite uneasy, doesn't it? Rest assured, you're not a weakling.

She gently caresses your face with her empty left hand.

Adela: You're strong enough to be its match. That's why you got injured all over. No matter what happened in the fog last night, Mad Shears didn't dare to keep chasing you after you left the fog. Nor did it dare to attack you when you were unconscious.

**end**

Adela: No, it fled before I came. I'm sure of it. Last night was your victory.

Adela: You're very strong. It's just that you can't remember—but that doesn't change who you are.

She gently straightens your shoulders and motions for you to look in the mirror.

Your haircut is done. Adela didn't make any drastic changes. She just trimmed it a little, hiding the gaping cuts made by "Mad Shears" with clever layering. You look quite spruced up.

The terrible snapping hallucinations gradually subside from your ears. You see your familiar old self in the mirror, even though you still can't remember a lot of things from the past.

After calming down, you make up your mind... Whatever your suspicions, this hair stylist just saved you right in front of "Mad Shears". It's a fact that she's not the same person as that monster.

Your tense muscles finally relax. Only then do you remember to let go of her hand. Gazing at her calm face, you actually come to admire this woman a bit.

**【Advance】**

- ...You're pretty impressive, too.

**end**

Chief: Everyone was freaking out after the street got blocked off, but you stayed as calm as ever.

Chief: You know full well of Mad Shears' existence, yet you still dared to venture out at night alone and save me from the fog. This isn't just an urban legend. You could've gotten hurt.

Adela: Yeah. I also lost something in the fog.

**【Choose One】**

- ...Is that all?

  She smiles faintly.

- Do you want to go find it?

  Adela ponders for a moment.

**end**

Adela: No need. Things are fine the way they are.

Adela: My life is as peaceful as always. I'm content. It should continue like this.

Adela: I have work tomorrow, so I can't help you. But you can explore the neighborhood after the sun comes up. The neighbors are all quite kind. Maybe someone has seen you before and can give you some clues.

She sweeps the hair on the floor and tidies up everything swiftly.

Adela: All right, go home now. You should get some rest. You're welcome to stay with me until you regain your memory.

Adela: Just relax now. The fog will clear up. You shouldn't worry about it.

## FOG-201 Investigation Begins

You awaken to a familiar touch and fragrance.

**【Advance】**

- ...Adela?

**end**

No response.

Yesterday's hair-raising encounter almost seemed like an illusion, but your memories are still missing. The reason why Mad Shears attacked you remains unclear.

The breakfast and the note on the table brings you back to reality.

The note is still in the same place as yesterday, but the breakfast has changed from meager to grand.

The eggs have been fried to a golden hue, boasting crispy outer layers and soft yolks that flow out as you bite into them. The bread is toasted in butter, giving off a rich, mouthwatering aroma.

**【Advance】**

- Open the note

**end**

"The fog got heavier. Don't go out.

I'll try to come home from work as early as possible."

"...Even if I say so, you'll probably still go out to seek the truth.

Go ahead, then. Just be careful not to end up in the fog. It's dangerous."

"The neighbors are all very nice people. You can ask them for help.

I'll be at the salon the whole day. You're welcome to come if you need anything.

Here's the spare key.

—Adela "

You see some ink seeping through the paper. There are more words on the back.

**【Advance】**

- Look on the back

**end**

"Remember to eat breakfast."

The sausages and bacon are rich in fat. The unique satiety of meat not only fills your stomach, but also lifts your spirit.

You really feel recharged after the meal.

You clear the table gratefully and plan out your next steps as you wash the dishes: retrieve your lost memories, then get to the bottom of this fog and the bizarre person in it.

Finally, you pick up the key she prepared for you and set off.

## FOG-L5 Lantern Ghost 1

You find yourself back in the Fog Alley, where you were attacked yesterday.

The only difference is that you're no longer surrounded by thick fog. And there are a couple of townies chatting nearby. You listen in on their conversation.

Townie A: Oh, have you folks encountered a talking lantern lately? It pauses mid-sentence randomly when it's speaking.

Townie A: Rumors about this spooky lantern are running wild. "The Lantern Ghost of Sloane Street," they call it.

Townie C: Hey! Why the hell did you bring that up? And in the middle of the night... It gives me the creeps just thinking about it.

Townie B: No, i-it's real. A friend of mine saw it. According to him, this freaking lantern just leaped out of nowhere and even asked him to play games with it.

The other two listen to the tale with their hearts in their mouths.

Townie B: He came out of the fog alive by following the light. But not so long ago, he was admitted to a mental hospital. It was very sudden.

The storyteller has turned ashen-faced.

Townie C: Stay away from dark alleys. Avoid them at all costs. Don't respond to any voice you hear in thick fog. Just run as fast as you can!

Townie A: Damn... is it that bad?

Townie C: If you don't run fast enough and get caught, you'll be tormented. Have you seen any of those videos posted online?

Townie B: That's right. If you're trapped in thick fog, you'll hear hysterical laughter and approaching footsteps, it'll keep tormenting you, until...

Townie A: Eehh!

The more they talked, the more evil the lantern became. It sounds like this fabled phantom is an evil thing that is haunting the town. Looks like you have gotten off easy. It saved your life, after all.

You want to share your story with the townies. You make to approach them, but stop in your tracks when you notice an antique lantern on the ground.

You pick it up and come up to them. The light from the lantern illuminates where they're standing.

You're about to say something when the lantern preempts you.

??: Hello... everyone...

The lantern frees itself from your grip and floats in the air. It starts talking, pausing mid-sentence randomly. It's the voice you heard in the fog just the other day.

??: Is there... anyone here... who wants to...

Townie A: Th-this is...

Townie C: Holy ****! Stop standing still! Run for it! Run!!!

Townie B: Don't look back! Or you'll be trapped forever!

The townspeople all ran and disappeared into the night.

??: ...

The floating lantern turns, and then stops, as if to face you.

??: It's... you?

??: Excellent!

Looks like it recognizes you.

??: Do you... want to play a game... with me?

After issuing the invitation, it starts circling slowly around you, as if sizing you up.

**【Choose One】**

- Sorry.

- I've got other things to deal with now.

**end**

You still haven't solved the mystery of the Foggy Night. It's no time to play games, not to mention with a ghost that's the subject of a horror story.

??: What if... what if...

??: I gave you... money? Would you play with me then?

**【Advance】**

- ...

**end**

??: Still a no?

??: But my dad told me... there's no problem in this world that money can't solve...

The ghost doesn't strike you as horrifying. Isn't this suppose to attack you and make you end up in a mental hospital?

And it must know something about Mad Shears, considering it can move freely in thick fog.

**【Advance】**

- Do you know anything about that strange person I saw yesterday?

**end**

??: Strange person?

The lantern does a little shake when it realizes who you're talking about.

??: Oh, you mean the lady carrying a big pair of scissors? She's, um, very special.

??: I wouldn't call her strange though.

You were right. It does know something.

It's strange to hear a lantern telling you what is strange and what is not.

**【Advance】**

- I'll play with you...

**end**

Lantern: Hooray! I've got myself a new playmate.

Chief: On one condition—that you tell me everything you know about Mad Shears.

Lantern: Hehe, will do, but only after our game.

The lighter flickers. A black mass flies out and molds itself into a shadow figure.

**【Advance】**

- What are we playing?

**end**

Black Shadow: Let's play hide-and-seek! But you win only if you catch me.

Yesterday's chasing game all over again? You decide to give it your best shot, since there's no other way to get the information you want.

Black Shadow: On the count of five... Are you ready?

You nod. You get set, ready to sprint.

Black Shadow: One...

Black Shadow: Two...

Black Shadow: Five!

With a flash of light from the lantern, the shadow figure has put a measurable distance between the two of you. After the initial surprise, you start chasing it.

## FOG-203 To the Bell Tower

But they don't cause any cuts.

Instead, the blade glides across the resident's body, spewing forth an equally familiar patch of dark contamination. It wriggles a bit and transforms into a familiar form—a terrifying monster made of tangled hair.

The root of all mysteries still lies with Mad Shears. It vanishes into the fog, heading towards the clock tower by the dark alley where you were attacked.

Unable to shake off her past, Andrea abandons her conscience. Socially awkward Ritz dumps his troublesome lover, and idealistic Kentor quits his job. All of them are no longer pestered by their past worries.

With your missing power?

With your incomplete memory?

Chief: There is a special connection between us. It takes a bit of time, but as long as the shackles are still here, they're sure to find me.

Chief: They are my cherished "troubles." I won't let you sever them from me.

Adela: ...I see... So you said all those things to stall time.

<ins>Adela: How clever of you, Mr. Inspector...</ins><br><ins>Adela: How clever of you, Ms. Inspector...</ins>

The constraint on you loosens. She seems to suddenly have lost all her strength. You finally claim the victory you deserve. However, after going through so much, you can no longer regard her simply as a hostile target.

On that first night, why did she fight you so fiercely? And after that, why didn't she take back her shears?

Adela: No need. Things are fine the way they are.

Adela: My life is as peaceful as always. I'm content. It should continue like this.

**【Advance】**

- That wasn't just for stalling time.

**end**

Chief: ...I really meant what I said.

Chief: I don't know how "Mad Shears" came about, or what happened in the past to make you so obsessed with "worries." But I'm willing to trust the gentle Adela I know.

Chief: I trust that you really want to help others, and that you want to cherish your stable and peaceful life in your own way.

Chief: Put down the shears. So long as they control you, you'll never get what you truly want.

Adela: A stable and peaceful life... yes, I do cherish a life like that.

Adela gets up slowly, lowering her shears. Under the strong light, you can't see her expression clearly.

Adela: I like the neighbors here. I like the worry-free days I've spent here.

Adela: After wandering for so long, I finally found... such a peaceful place.

An ominous feeling appears. The voices of the townspeople ring out from the thinning fog.

Resident: The FAC! It's the FAC! We're saved! The fog is about to get dispersed! Mad Shears is at... the hair salon?

Resident: Isn't that Adela's shop? How is Miss Adela? Why are those people pointing their guns at her?

Resident: ...No, that's impossible... How can Adela be Mad Shears? Adela, she...

The thick fog is surging slowly, and more and more murmurs pass through it to reach your ears. Under the bright light, the notorious Mad Shears of Eastside has nowhere to hide, and looks a bit dazed.

Adela: Hair... So much hair... Everyone is worried again... and this time, it's because of me...

Adela: Hehe... This moment would come sooner or later...

Her figure is so frail that it looks like she's going to disappear into the light at any moment.

**【Advance】**

- Adela?

**end**

You're suddenly seized by panic. You have to grab her! Out of instinct, you ignore the danger and struggle up, trying to grab her.

**【Advance】**

- Don't do anything stupid!

**end**

Chief: Things aren't hopeless yet! You're just a victim of circumstance who got controlled by Mania. Everyone will understand! I'll... try to bail you out and let you come back here.

You reach out to her, but grab her shears instead. In an instant, a head-splitting headache hits you again. Your vision quivers, blocked by long messy hair once more.

The hair belongs to Adela this time. It has grown to enormous lengths, tangled and heavy.

Adela: I haven't experienced this for so long that I almost forgot... this is what worry feels like...

Adela: I moved many times before finally finding this place—a little home where I can sleep peacefully, a job that I can excel at, and the opportunity to gain trust and give back. Every problem has its solution.

**【Advance】**

- I don't want to take away what you cherish.

**end**

You want to pull her to your side, but the intense disorientation caused by the shears makes it impossible to do so.

<ins>Adela: It's fine. Don't worry about me, Mr. Inspector. No one will get hurt...</ins><br><ins>Adela: It's fine. Don't worry about me, Miss Inspector. No one will get hurt...</ins>

Adela: I'm a hairdresser who trims away worries. Dealing with this kind of thing is my forte.

To be the Mad Shears who goes around Eastside doing as she pleased without ever revealing her face, while at the same time being worry-free Adela...

How is that possible? It's not that she's never been discovered, or that she doesn't have any worries, it's just that she knows how to deal with those things.

Under the aim of countless guns, Adela lets go of your hand gently.

Adela: Farewell, ${PlayerName}.

Adela: Whether it's Adela or Mad Shears... You don't have to worry about me any longer.

**【Advance】**

- Grab her

**end**

You've failed. The hair on the floor ensnares you. Adela takes a step back, clutching the shears in her hand. You hear the alarm go off nearby.

Arrest Team Captain: The M-value is rising! What the... Her power is growing rapidly! Be careful, everyone! It's an S-Class Sinner!!!

Nightingale: ?! Sniper, shoot!!! Stop her!!!

Bullets pierce her shadow, riddling the ground with holes. Dense fog gushes forth, blanketing the entire area once again.

Chief: Stop, Adela! Stop...

With a cold flicker, the blade slashes you. Something flashes across your eyes. You're alive, but something in your consciousness has splintered off again.

Nightingale: Chief!! Argh!

Her shadow flashes by. The helicopters shudder violently, and some of them crash to the ground.

You struggle to crawl over and force yourself into the cabin. Nightingale, the combatants, and even the Sinners... They're all fine, but have lost consciousness.

**【Advance】**

- Adela? Where are you?

**end**

Chief: Adela...

You open your mouth, but forget what you wanted to shout. Your memory is shattered once more. You seem to hear the townspeople's screams in the distance, wave after wave. Soon after, everything falls into silence again. All that is left is the rolling fog.

But you manage to remember something. You reach into your pocket and feel a string of keys.

...To find her, you must go to that place. There's still a chance. She's not without worries. She knows what it's like to suffer from them, and what it's like to cherish them. She can't bear to part with them, either.

You must catch her... while you still can.

## FOG-304 Forget

You stop at the center of the fog—before the door of a familiar house.

**【Advance】**

- Open the door and enter

**end**

This is an illusory space. Thick fog permeates the room, and all her once neatly arranged items are floating in the air. You vaguely recall its original appearance.

The soft mattress, the clean clothing, the delicious breakfast—you still have all these memories, yet they're fading quickly to the soothing fragrance.

You have to find her quickly before you forget everything.

**【Advance】**

- That locked suitcase.

**end**

The suitcase is gone, perhaps taken away by its owner. That little suitcase can't hold much. Its owner left in a big hurry.

...Or perhaps, without anything to long for.

You hear a sound. Clinging on to the last bit of hope, you find the room where the sound came from.

**【Advance】**

- Knock on the door

**end**

The door is unlocked, and creaks open from your movements.

You see familiar long hair.

Blurry Shadow: I'm a hairdresser who trims away worries. Dealing with this kind of thing is my forte.

A shadow made of hair lies at the center, murmuring quietly. Its voice is hoarse, with a hint of sadness.

Hairy Shadow: Must I go... Must I go...

Hairy Shadow: But I...

Hairy Shadow: Want to stay...

This is the heart of the fog. The shadow is made of Mad Shears' worries, which she has abandoned.

Operation Sloane Street has ended.

None of the victims were physically harmed. All recovered after some rest and are in stable mental condition.

Failed to detain Sinner suspect.

All electronic equipment at the scene were destroyed, leaving no visual data of the suspect.

Conclusion from analyzing Mania field remnants: S-Class, extremely dangerous.

The power is speculated to be consciousness manipulation, which is quite rare. High degree of vigilance required.

Hallucinations and amnesia are merely symptoms. Underlying cognition interference cannot be ruled out. If left uncontrolled, serious aftereffects may ensue.

No traces were left at the hair salon in question. The investigator found a photo album full of happily smiling customers.

Upon interviewing each one, not a single person remembers the place or who took their smiling picture.

??: ...Chief, Chief!

Hella: Hey dummy, you finally woke up! Remember who I am?

**【Advance】**

- ...Hella? Hecate?

**end**

You remember them. You remember everything.

Nightingale: The medical team has arrived and will conduct thorough examinations for both the residents and you. We must not underestimate the enemy's power.

**【Advance】**

- ...Where's the enemy?

**end**

Nightingale: Defeated by you. You have destroyed the monster and saved the street.

Nightingale: The fog has lifted, Chief.

You're surrounded by your Sinners. Time, which had been frozen by the fog, starts to flow again. The sun shines upon this street once more. You can all leave now.

Yet a forlorn feeling lingers in your heart.

You're still clutching a key in your hand, which gives off a familiar soothing yet melancholy fragrance.

**【Advance】**

- It's not my key, it's...

**end**

Chief: Whose is it?

## The Phantom of the Mist - Boss Challenge

### ACT-3-The Phantom of the Mist - Boss Challenge

#### Nucleus of Worry

> All the troubles and memories are cut and completely abandoned in the fog. Chief will challenge the Nucleus of Worry made of countless strands of hair, to bring this event to a close.

## The Phantom of the Mist - EX Stage

### ACT-3-The Phantom of the Mist - EX Stage

#### EX-FOG-001 Lonely Lantern Twist

> In the thick fog, even the brightest streetlights become obscure. Or was the light itself a vapor created by the darkness?

#### EX-FOG-002 A Tale of Two Alleys

> The night falls and the fog rises. Dark hair spreads in all directions, devouring the buildings it crawls past. The alley feels utterly familiar, yet it provokes the deepest fear.

#### EX-FOG-003 Gaiden of Sloane

> Sloane Street harbors many secrets, and everyone shares them with their barber. Perhaps the Sloane Street looks very different to the barber.

#### EX-FOG-004 Old Hair Salon

> Legend has it that there is an old hair salon here, and no one remembers exactly how long it has been there. All people know is that they like and trust the current owner of the hair salon.

#### EX-FOG-005 Ode to Darkness

> Finally, you discover the deepest worry.
> Finally, you step into the Maze in the Fog.
> Now, follow me, bow your head and praise this chaotic darkness.
